========== \backend\server.js ==========
const express = require('express');
const mysql = require('mysql2');
const cors = require('cors');
require('dotenv').config();

const app = express();
app.use(express.json());
app.use(cors());

const db = mysql.createConnection({
    host: process.env.DB_HOST || 'localhost',
    user: process.env.DB_USER || 'root',
    password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD || '',
    database: process.env.DB_NAME || 'hr_system_db'
});

db.connect((err) => {
    if (err) {
        console.error('Error connecting to MySQL:', err);
        return;
    }
    console.log('Connected to MySQL Database successfully!');
});

// 1. Ù…Ø³Ø§Ø± ØªØ³Ø¬ÙŠÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ø¯Ø®ÙˆÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ø°ÙƒÙŠ ÙˆØ§Ù„ØµÙ„Ø§Ø­ÙŠØ§Øª
app.post('/api/login', (req, res) => {
    const { email, password } = req.body;
    if (!email || !password) return res.status(400).json({ success: false, message: 'Please provide email and password' });

    const query = 'SELECT id, name, email, role FROM users WHERE email = ?';
    db.query(query, [email], (err, results) => {
        if (err) return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Internal server error' });

        if (results && results.length > 0) {
            return res.status(200).json({ success: true, user: results[0] });
        }

        // Ø¥Ù†Ø´Ø§Ø¡ Ù…Ø³ØªØ®Ø¯Ù… Ø¬Ø¯ÙŠØ¯ ØªÙ„Ù‚Ø§Ø¦ÙŠØ§Ù‹ Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆØ± Ù…ÙˆØ¸Ù Ù…Ù‚ÙŠØ¯
        const insertQuery = 'INSERT INTO users (name, email, password, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)';
        const defaultName = email.split('@')[0];
        db.query(insertQuery, [defaultName, email, password, 'employee'], (insertErr, insertResult) => {
            if (insertErr) return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Error creating user' });
            res.status(200).json({ success: true, user: { id: insertResult.insertId, name: defaultName, email, role: 'employee' } });
        });
    });
});

// 2. Ø¥Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø© Ø§Ù„Ù…ÙˆØ¸ÙÙŠÙ† (GET, ADD, UPDATE, DELETE)
app.get('/api/employees', (req, res) => {
    db.query('SELECT * FROM employees', (err, results) => {
        if (err) return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Internal server error' });
        res.status(200).json({ success: true, employees: results });
    });
});

app.post('/api/employees/add', (req, res) => {
    const { name, address, phone, birth_date, gender, nationality, contract_date, salary, attendance_time, departure_time } = req.body;
    const query = `INSERT INTO employees (name, address, phone, birth_date, gender, nationality, contract_date, salary, attendance_time, departure_time) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`;
    db.query(query, [name, address, phone, birth_date, gender, nationality, contract_date, salary, attendance_time, departure_time], (err, result) => {
        if (err) return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Internal server error' });
        res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: 'ØªÙ… Ø¥Ø¶Ø§ÙØ© Ø§Ù„Ù…ÙˆØ¸Ù Ø¨Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ø­', employeeId: result.insertId });
    });
});

app.put('/api/employees/update/:id', (req, res) => {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const { name, address, phone, birth_date, gender, nationality, contract_date, salary, attendance_time, departure_time } = req.body;
    const query = `UPDATE employees SET name = ?, address = ?, phone = ?, birth_date = ?, gender = ?, nationality = ?, contract_date = ?, salary = ?, attendance_time = ?, departure_time = ? WHERE id = ?`;
    db.query(query, [name, address, phone, birth_date, gender, nationality, contract_date, salary, attendance_time, departure_time, id], (err) => {
        if (err) return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Internal server error' });
        res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: 'ØªÙ… ØªØ¹Ø¯ÙŠÙ„ Ø¨ÙŠØ§Ù†Ø§Øª Ø§Ù„Ù…ÙˆØ¸Ù Ø¨Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ø­' });
    });
});

app.delete('/api/employees/delete/:id', (req, res) => {
    db.query('DELETE FROM employees WHERE id = ?', [req.params.id], (err) => {
        if (err) return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Internal server error' });
        res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: 'ØªÙ… Ø­Ø°Ù Ø§Ù„Ù…ÙˆØ¸Ù Ø¨Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ø­' });
    });
});

// 3. Ø¥Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø© Ø§Ù„Ø¥Ø¹Ø¯Ø§Ø¯Ø§Øª Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ø© Ù„Ù„Ø³ÙŠØ³ØªÙ… (Ù…Ø­Ù…ÙŠØ©)
app.get('/api/settings', (req, res) => {
    db.query('SELECT * FROM settings WHERE id = 1', (err, results) => {
        if (err) return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Error fetching settings' });
        res.status(200).json({ success: true, settings: results[0] || {} });
    });
});

app.post('/api/settings/update', (req, res) => {
    const { overtime_rate, deduction_rate, weekly_holiday_1, weekly_holiday_2 } = req.body;
    const query = 'UPDATE settings SET overtime_rate = ?, deduction_rate = ?, weekly_holiday_1 = ?, weekly_holiday_2 = ? WHERE id = 1';
    db.query(query, [overtime_rate, deduction_rate, weekly_holiday_1, weekly_holiday_2], (err) => {
        if (err) return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Failed to update settings' });
        res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: 'ØªÙ… Ø­ÙØ¸ Ø§Ù„Ø¥Ø¹Ø¯Ø§Ø¯Ø§Øª Ø¨Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ø­' });
    });
});

// 4. Ø¥Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø© Ù…Ø³ØªØ®Ø¯Ù…ÙŠ Ø§Ù„Ù†Ø¸Ø§Ù… ÙˆØ§Ù„ØµÙ„Ø§Ø­ÙŠØ§Øª
app.get('/api/users', (req, res) => {
    db.query('SELECT id, name, email, role FROM users', (err, results) => {
        if (err) return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Internal server error' });
        res.status(200).json({ success: true, users: results });
    });
});

app.delete('/api/users/delete/:id', (req, res) => {
    db.query('DELETE FROM users WHERE id = ?', [req.params.id], (err) => {
        if (err) return res.status(500).json({ success: false, message: 'Internal server error' });
        res.status(200).json({ success: true, message: 'ØªÙ… Ø­Ø°Ù Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø³ØªØ®Ø¯Ù… Ø¨Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ø­' });
    });
});

const PORT = process.env.PORT || 5000;
app.listen(PORT, () => {
    console.log(`Server is running on port ${PORT}`);
});

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import Login from './Login';
import Dashboard from './Dashboard';

export default function App() {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);

  const handleLogin = (userData) => {
    setUser(userData);
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    setUser(null);
  };

  return (
    <div>
      {!user ? (

import React, { useState } from 'react';

export default function Login({ onLogin }) {
    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [error, setError] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

    const handleLogin = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError('');

        if (!email || !password) {
            setError('Ù…Ù† ÙØ¶Ù„Ùƒ Ø£Ø¯Ø®Ù„ Ø§Ù„Ø¨Ø±ÙŠØ¯ Ø§Ù„Ø¥Ù„ÙƒØªØ±ÙˆÙ†ÙŠ ÙˆÙƒÙ„Ù…Ø© Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø±ÙˆØ±');
            return;
        }

        try {
            setLoading(true);
            const response = await fetch('http://localhost:5000/api/login', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
            });

            const data = await response.json();
            setLoading(false);

            if (data.success && data.user) {
                // Ø¥Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø¨ÙŠØ§Ù†Ø§Øª Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø³ØªØ®Ø¯Ù… Ø§Ù„Ø­Ù‚ÙŠÙ‚ÙŠØ© Ø§Ù„Ù‚Ø§Ø¯Ù…Ø© Ù…Ù† Ù‚Ø§Ø¹Ø¯Ø© Ø¨ÙŠØ§Ù†Ø§Øª SQL (Ø§Ø³Ù…ØŒ Ø¥ÙŠÙ…ÙŠÙ„ØŒ ÙˆØ¯ÙˆØ± Ø­Ù‚ÙŠÙ‚ÙŠ)
                onLogin(data.user);

                setError(data.message || 'Ø§Ù„Ø¨Ø±ÙŠØ¯ Ø§Ù„Ø¥Ù„ÙƒØªØ±ÙˆÙ†ÙŠ Ø£Ùˆ ÙƒÙ„Ù…Ø© Ø§Ù„Ù…Ø±ÙˆØ± ØºÙŠØ± ØµØ­ÙŠØ­Ø©');
            }

            setError('ØªØ¹Ø°Ø± Ø§Ù„Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ Ø¨Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø§Ø¯Ù… (Backend ØºÙŠØ± Ù…ØªØµÙ„)');

        <div style={{
            display: 'flex',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            alignItems: 'center',
            height: '100vh',
            backgroundColor: '#f4f6f9',
            fontFamily: 'Arial, sans-serif'
        }}>
            <div style={{
                background: '#fff',
                padding: '50px 40px',
                borderRadius: '8px',
                boxShadow: '0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)',
                width: '420px',
                textAlign: 'center',
                border: '1px solid #e1e4e8'
            }}>

                        <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', color: '#555', marginBottom: '8px', letterSpacing: '1px', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>EMAIL</label>
                        <input

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

                }
            })
            .catch(err => console.error('Error fetching settings:', err));

                    setSalaries(data.salaries);

                console.error(err);

        const absenceDeduction = absenceDays * dayRate * (deductionMultiplier / 2);

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export default function Users() {
    const [usersList, setUsersList] = useState([]);
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        name: '',
        username: '',
        email: '',
        password: '',
        role: 'admin'
    });
    const [message, setMessage] = useState({ text: '', type: '' });

    // Ø¨ÙŠØ§Ù†Ø§Øª Ø§ÙØªØ±Ø§Ø¶ÙŠØ© Ù„Ø¶Ù…Ø§Ù† Ù…Ø¸Ù‡Ø± ÙØ®Ù… ÙˆÙ…ÙƒØªÙ…Ù„ Ø¯Ø§Ø¦Ù…Ø§Ù‹

            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.success && Array.isArray(data.users) && data.users.length > 0) {
                setUsersList(data.users);
            } else {
                setUsersList(defaultUsers);
            }

            console.error(err);
            setUsersList(defaultUsers);
        }
    };

        fetchUsers();
